		function PriceReqMod() {
			urlPriceReq = urlPriceReq.replace('&amp;','&').replace('%28','(').replace('%2d','-').replace('%29',')');
			urlPriceReq = urlPriceReq.replace('http','https');
			urlPriceReq = urlPriceReq.replace('httpss','https');
		}

<h2 class="img_startseite_headline"><span class="campaign_markup">//</span>Nuestras novedades de un vistazo</h2>
<div class="clear"></div>
<div class="news_box_gesamt" name="StartInfoboxen">
<div class="neos-contentcollection">
<div class="onlineprinters-website-newslist" id="node-58106a5f937a6">
<div class="onlineprinters-website-newsteaser" id="node-ftae9oru5x2ka">
<div class="teaser-headline">
<a href="https://www.onlineprinters.es/c/nosotros/actualidad/calidad-triple"><img src="https://d2slpf6wny259j.cloudfront.net/_Resources/Persistent/a93eb9dd3bae3880652258f6b02697d5e68d6329/News_mini_ES.jpg"/></a>
<div>
<h3><a href="https://www.onlineprinters.es/c/nosotros/actualidad/calidad-triple">Única en Europa: triple calidad</a></h3>
</div>
</div>
<p>
		Onlineprinters es la primera imprenta online de Europa en obtener una triple certificación para las áreas de impresión offset, impresión digital y tratamiento posterior. 
		<a href="https://www.onlineprinters.es/c/nosotros/actualidad/calidad-triple">[más]</a>
</p>
</div>
<div class="onlineprinters-website-newsteaser" id="node-x3b00zoprvs2t">
<div class="teaser-headline">
<a href="https://www.onlineprinters.es/c/nosotros/actualidad/nuevo-transportista-UPS"><img src="https://d2slpf6wny259j.cloudfront.net/_Resources/Persistent/7ffc7f13ce2550d67246bcdfd892f44c7b0d560c/News_UPS_ES.jpg"/></a>
<div>
<h3><a href="https://www.onlineprinters.es/c/nosotros/actualidad/nuevo-transportista-UPS">Nuevo transportista: desde el 18/09 envío exprés por UPS</a></h3>
</div>
</div>
<p>
		Hemos optimizado para ti nuestro envío exprés. A partir del 18 de septiembre de 2019, la empresa de transportes UPS asumirá la entrega puntual de los paquetes exprés. 
		<a href="https://www.onlineprinters.es/c/nosotros/actualidad/nuevo-transportista-UPS">[más]</a>
</p>
</div>
<div class="onlineprinters-website-newsteaser" id="node-nrf4k17zqrdj1">
<div class="teaser-headline">
<a href="https://www.onlineprinters.es/c/nosotros/actualidad/Nuevo-produccion-economica-de-catalogos"><img src="https://d2slpf6wny259j.cloudfront.net/_Resources/Persistent/ea173ef1d63bbc658eae3017ed16936b8fde6ed4/Budget.jpg"/></a>
<div>
<h3><a href="https://www.onlineprinters.es/c/nosotros/actualidad/Nuevo-produccion-economica-de-catalogos">Nuevo: producción económica de catálogos</a></h3>
</div>
</div>
<p>
		Además de nuestros clásicos catálogos para imprimir varias imágenes, también te ofrecemos una alternativa económica para impresos con mucho texto como instrucciones de uso: catálogos económicos. 
		<a href="https://www.onlineprinters.es/c/nosotros/actualidad/Nuevo-produccion-economica-de-catalogos">[más]</a>
</p>
</div>
</div></div>
<div class="box_pr_descr" id="archiv_link">
					 <a href="https://www.onlineprinters.es/c/nosotros/actualidad/archivo" style="">Archivo de noticias</a>.
				</div>
</div>
</div>

<script type="text/javascript">
    
    
        $(window).on('resize scroll', function() {
            $(window).on('load',function() {   
             var element =  $('.marketingTeaserWrapper-active-slide'),
                 index = $('.opidMarketingTeaserSliderWrapper').index(element);
           
                if(ga360Obj.isInViewport(element)) {
                    if($.inArray(index, ga360Obj.visitedTabs) === -1) {
                        ga360Obj.visitedTabs.push(index);
                        ga360Obj.getPromotionData(element, 'impression');
                    }
                }
            });
        });

<!-- start Incl-LiveChatKontaktwidget -->
<script type="text/javascript">
			// Nicht umwandeln in ein Scripttag -> bremst die Ladezeit um bis zu 0,5s aus. Deswegen wird das Userlikeskript erst onload geladen.
			$(window).on('load',function(){
				$.getScript('//userlike-cdn-widgets.s3-eu-west-1.amazonaws.com/dd218641a19d2ca274236695c18169d0c0f3ae2f5bd9c8c792e748f8b8e7cef7.js');
			});

			var proactiveModeProducts = [/*Plakate*/'001408','001409','001415','001412','011520','011964','011923','001411','002249','001414','001419','002472','002471',/*Broschüren*/'001445','001446','009976','008347','001448','001449','001450','001451','001452',/*RollupDisplays*/'008946','008947','020328','020330','020332','020338','009336','020336','020334','020340','008948'];
			function getChatMetadata()
			{
				var chatMetadata = {firstname: "",lastname: "",email: "", extid: ''};

				if(!sessionStorage)
					return chatMetadata;
				
				if(sessionStorage.getItem('opChatMetadata') == null)
					return chatMetadata;
				
				try
				{
					return JSON.parse(atob(sessionStorage.getItem('opChatMetadata')));
				}
				catch(e)
				{
					return chatMetadata;
				}
			}
			function getChatMode()
			{
				if(oipa == "true") //interne IP-Adresse von onlineprinters
					return 'STANDARD';
					
				if(localStorage && localStorage.getItem('CHAT_PROACTIVE_WINDOW_CLOSED') === "true")
					return 'STANDARD';

				if(['Artikel','Kategorie'].indexOf(window.ws_page_name) != -1 && proactiveModeProducts.indexOf(ws_cat_index) != -1)
					return 'PROACTIVE';

				if(window.ws_page_name == "Bestellung")
					return 'PROACTIVE';

				return "STANDARD";
			}

			function userIsBlocked(){
				var userlikeCookie = oplib.getCookie('uslk_e');
				if(userlikeCookie == "")
					return false;
				
				var cookieParsed = userlikeCookie.split('~');
				var isBlocked = cookieParsed[1] == "1";
				return isBlocked;
			}

			function kontaktwidgetChatOnline(){
				$('#chatWidgetTab.contactWidget').addClass('activ');
				$('#chatWidgetTab.contactWidget').removeClass('inactiv');
			}
			function kontaktwidgetChatOffline(){
				$('#chatWidgetTab.contactWidget').addClass('inactiv');
				$('#chatWidgetTab.contactWidget').removeClass('activ');
			}

			function userlikeCheckChatStatus(){

				if(userIsBlocked())
				{
					kontaktwidgetChatOffline();
					return; 
				}
				//Wir benÃ¶tigen den Status des Chats - also verfÃ¼gbar oder nicht verfÃ¼gbar. Diesen bekommen wir Ã¼ber einen JSONP-Request
				oplib.jsonpRequest({
					jsonpCallback: 'chatstatus',
					url: "https://api.userlike.com/api/chat/slot/available/?customer_id=32691&chat_widget_id=63434&callback=chatstatus",
					timeout: 1000
				}, 
				function (result){
					var widgetStatus = userlike.userlikeChatState();  //MÃ¶glich: "Idle", "Chat", "Offline"
					var apistatus = result.message;
					var userlikeWindowOpen = $('#userlike-popup').length > 0;

					if(widgetStatus == "ChatMinimized" && apistatus == "NO_SLOT" ){
						kontaktwidgetChatOnline();
					}
					else if(widgetStatus == "Offline" && apistatus == "GROUP_ONLINE" && userlikeWindowOpen ){
						kontaktwidgetChatOnline();
					}
					else if(widgetStatus == "Offline" && apistatus == "GROUP_ONLINE" ){
						kontaktwidgetChatOffline();
					}
					else if(widgetStatus == "Chat" || apistatus == "GROUP_ONLINE")
					{
						kontaktwidgetChatOnline();
					}
					else if(widgetStatus == "Idle" && (apistatus == "GROUP_OFFLINE" || apistatus == "NO_SLOT") ){
						kontaktwidgetChatOffline();
					}	
					else if(widgetStatus == "Idle" ){
						kontaktwidgetChatOnline();
					}	
					else{
						kontaktwidgetChatOffline()
					}
				});
			}

			var randomName = Math.random().toString(36).substring(2,7);
			var chatMetadata = getChatMetadata();
			var extid = chatMetadata.extid || Math.random().toString(36).substr(2) + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2);
			var loginUsername						= " ";
			var loginUsermail						= "";
			var userIndex 							= "0" == "0" ? "" : "0";
			var wcUsername							= "".trim();
			var firstname							= "".trim() || chatMetadata.firstname;
			var lastname							= "".trim() || chatMetadata.lastname || randomName;
			var wcUsermail							= "".trim() || chatMetadata.email;
			var username = loginUsername.trim() || wcUsername.trim() || (firstname + " " + lastname).trim();
			var email = loginUsermail.trim() || wcUsermail.trim() || "";

			
			function userlikeTrackingEvent(name, global_ctx, session_ctx)
			{
				switch(name)
				{
					case 'button_displayed':
					case 'button_hidden':
						if(sessionStorage.getItem('lastchatevent') !== name)
						{
							emos3.send({Target: ['Chat',name,1,'a']});
							sessionStorage.setItem('lastchatevent',name);
						}
						break;
					case 'chat_requested': 
					case 'pre_survey_submit': 
					case 'chat_started': 
					case 'message_client_terminating_first':
					case 'chat_window_minimized': 
					case 'chat_quit': 
					case 'error_message': 
					case 'proactive_offer': 
					case 'chat_started_proactive': 
					case 'proactive_reject': 

					emos3.send({Target: ['Chat',name,1,'a']});
					break;
				}
			} 

			function userlikeCallbackMessageReceived()
			{
				if(userlike.userlikeChatState() == "ChatMinimized")
					userlike.userlikeStartChat();
			}
			
			function userlikeRemoteReady() {
				var chatMode = getChatMode();
			
				if(chatMode == "PROACTIVE")
					userlike.userlikeRemoteApiModeProactivePassive(10);
				else
					userlike.userlikeRemoteApiModeNormal();
			};  

			// usrelikeReady wird nicht aufgerufen, wenn ein Benutzer blockiert ist. Deshalb muss es hier nochmals stehen.
			if(userIsBlocked())
			{
				kontaktwidgetChatOffline();
			}

			function userlikeReady(){
				var chatData = {
					user: { 
						name: username,
						email: email,
						additional02: opWsVars.isPremiumKunde ? "Ja" : "Nein",
					},
					custom: {
						extid: extid,
						Vorname: firstname,
						Nachname: lastname,
						Email: email,
						SubShopID: window.opWsVars.subShopId,
						UserIndex: userIndex,
						languageIETF: "es-ES" 
					}
				};

				if(oipa == "true") //interne IP-Adresse von onlineprinters
				{
					chatData.custom.isFromOnlineprinters = true;
				}

				/*
					Ãœbertrage die Metadaten an Userlike und speichere diese lokal, damit der User zukÃ¼nftig identifiziert werden kann.
					Wenn der Chat bereits lÃ¤uft, dann Daten nur Ã¼berschreiben, wenn der Kunde eingeloggt ist.
				*/
				if(! (userlike.userlikeChatState() == "Chat" && loginUsername.trim() == "") )
				{		
					userlike.setData(chatData);
					sessionStorage.setItem('opChatMetadata',btoa(JSON.stringify({firstname: firstname, lastname: lastname, email: email, extid: extid})));
				}

				userlikeCheckChatStatus();
				/*
					Merke das SchlieÃŸen des Chatfensters, damit das Fenster bei Vielnutzer nicht dauernd geÃ¶ffnet wird.
				*/
				$('#userlike-close').on('click',function(){
					if(getChatMode() == 'PROACTIVE' && localStorage)
					{
						localStorage.setItem('CHAT_PROACTIVE_WINDOW_CLOSED',true);
					}
				});

				/*
					Aktualisiere den Status des Chatpanels, wenn darÃ¼ber gehovert wird
				*/
				$('.contactWidgetWrapper').on('mouseenter mouseleave', _.debounce(userlikeCheckChatStatus,500));

				/*
					Starte den Chat, wenn auf den Chatreiter im Kontaktwidget geklickt wurde und tracke dies
				*/
				$('#chatWidgetTab').on('click', function(){
					if(userlike.userlikeChatState() == "Chat")
						return;

					var chatstatusActive = $('#chatWidgetTab.contactWidget.activ').length > 0;

					if(chatstatusActive)
					{
						userlike.userlikeStartChat();
						//Tracke den Klick, wenn Chat verfÃ¼gbar
						    dataLayer.push({ event:'click', eventAction: 'KontaktWidget Chatstart' });
						return;
					}	
					
					$('#userlike-close').click();
				});

				/*
					FÃ¼ge das Klickevent zum Abschicken der Nachrichten auf den Bereich des Absendenbuttons
				*/
				$('#userlike-chat-scroll-textarea').on('click', function(e){
					var klicked = false;

					if(e.offsetX < $(this).width() - 40 || e.offsetX > $(this).width() - 10)
						klicked = false;
					else if(e.offsetY < $(this).height() - 35 || e.offsetY > $(this).height() - 2)
						klicked = false;
					else
						klicked = true;

					if(klicked){
						userlike.userlikeInputEnter();
					}
				})

				/*
					Highlight den Chatbutton, wenn Text eingegeben wurde
				*/
				$('#userlike-chat-textarea').on('keyup', function(e){
					var defaultMessage = $('#userlike-chat-textarea').data('default');
					var textareaContent = $('#userlike-chat-textarea').val();
					var highlightSendButton = textareaContent != defaultMessage && textareaContent.length > 0;

					if(highlightSendButton)
						$('#userlike-chat-scroll-textarea').addClass('highlight');
					else
						$('#userlike-chat-scroll-textarea').removeClass('highlight');
				})

				$(document).on('loginFinished',function(){
					var email = $('.opidChatData').data('email');
					var firstname = $('.opidChatData').data('firstname');
					var lastname = $('.opidChatData').data('lastname');
					var userindex = $('.opidChatData').data('userindex');

					var newChatData = {
						user: {
							name: firstname + " " + lastname,
							email: email
						},
						custom: {
							UserIndex: userindex, 
							Email: email, 
							Vorname: firstname, 
							Nachname: lastname
						} 
					};
					userlike.setData(newChatData);
					userlike.userlikeUpdateAPI();
				})
			}
		</script>
